Second, a semantic soft-grouping module turns globally learnable Hawkes priors into an analytical cross-attention mask, so chains share excitation patterns through their latent group memberships rather than through exhaustive pairwise computation. Third, a Non-Crossing Quantile (NCQ) regression head replaces mean-based time prediction, providing calibrated, monotonically ordered quantile estimates that remain stable under heavy-tailed inter-arrival distributions. On six benchmark TKG datasets, GAttNHP improves over state-of-the-art baselines on both entity prediction and time prediction, and ablations confirm that its largest gains arise on the long-tail event chains where existing models fail most severely.

</details>


### [139] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时态知识图谱问题生成（TKGQG），提出首个具有时态表达能力和跳数约束的基准构建框架ChronoQG，生成16,011个验证问题，实验表明现有方法在保持时态约束方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQG基准基于静态知识图谱构建，无法编码图事实的时态范围，因此无法评估生成问题是否忠实保留时态有效性、事件顺序和答案决定性的时态约束。

Method: 提出ChronoQG框架，包含时态约束分类体系、拓扑-时态子图采样和基于轨迹的问题生成。从异构时态知识图谱构建四个基准数据集，共16,011个验证问题。

Result: 评估多种LLM-based KGQG方法和提示基线，发现现有方法在保持时态约束方面表现不佳，尤其是在多约束设置和难度更高的时态约束类型下差距更明显。

Conclusion: 揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间存在明显差距，ChronoQG可作为时态忠实问题生成领域的一个具有挑战性的测试基准。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

</details>


### [140] [Evaluating Epistemic Uncertainty: Beyond OOD Detection and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14817)
*Jakub Paplhám,Willem Waegeman,Eyke Hüllermeier,Vojtěch Franc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于认知拒绝选项框架，证明了选择性预测的最优选择器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合，并揭示了现有不确定性解耦文献中相关性指标无法预测实际效用的缺陷，提出用风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面来评估解耦与实用性。实验表明基于决策理论的排序与代理任务排序存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前认知不确定性的评估依赖于分布外检测和主动学习等任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用的认知不确定性评分不一致，现有评估方法无法真正衡量认知不确定性识别可约误差（遗憾）的能力。

Method: 基于认知拒绝选项框架，将选择性预测建模为覆盖度、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题，证明最优选择器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合；提出用可达到的风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面作为解耦与实用性的联合诊断指标。

Result: 标准相关性指标无法预测解耦组件的实际操作效用；在密集人工标注数据集上的基准测试显示，基于决策理论的排序与代理任务排序存在显著分歧，包括方法间的成对排名反转。

Conclusion: 现有不确定性解耦评估方法存在根本性缺陷，决策理论框架下的评估更能反映不确定性量化的实际效用，应取代基于代理任务的评估范式。

Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [141] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称峰值感知损失（APAL），一种针对时间序列预测中欠预测惩罚更重、峰值区域训练权重更高的损失函数，并配套峰值关键评估协议。在行人需求预测等任务中，APAL显著提升尾部精度和峰值预测质量，适用于峰值预测失败是主要运营风险的实际场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型多采用对称损失函数（如MSE、MAE）并以聚合误差评估，这掩盖了极端值和峰值时序预测的失败。而在许多实际应用（如人群需求预测）中，欠预测风险远高于过预测，准确预测罕见需求峰值对下游任务至关重要。

Method: 提出Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL)，包含两个关键设计：(i) 对欠预测施加更重惩罚；(ii) 提高每个预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出峰值关键评估协议，在MAE/MSE基础上补充通道级尾部误差（Top-10%和Top-1%）及峰值指标（精度、召回率、时序容差下的F1和峰值时序误差）。

Result: 在五个最先进骨干模型上，使用墨尔本行人逐时计数数据集（含生产级子集）和海滩访客计数数据集进行长时域多变量预测评估。APAL在峰值关键数据集和设置下提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量，同时暴露了与聚合误差之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: APAL是一种简单、模型无关的实用解决方案，适用于峰值预测失败是主要运营关注点的场景，能够有效平衡峰值预测精度与整体聚合误差。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [142] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了回合制随机博弈（TBSG）中可达性目标的去中心化、私有信息学习问题，首次在不需要双方共享学习算法或信息的假设下取得了正面结果，并提出了期望条件距离（ECD）参数的多项式样本复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达性目标是强化学习中最基本的逻辑目标，但在马尔可夫决策过程和TBSG中，PAC学习在无额外假设下是不可能的。现有工作假设双方共享公共信息和集中式学习算法，这些假设过强，需要放松以实现更实际的学习场景。

Method: 将期望条件距离（ECD）参数推广到博弈论设置，用于衡量到达目标集的期望步长。在去中心化（双方使用不同学习算法）和私有信息（信息不共享）假设下，建立可达性目标PAC学习的样本复杂度上界。

Result: 首次在TBSG中去中心化、私有信息学习设置下取得可达性目标学习的正面结果；建立了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数、误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 通过放松公共信息和集中式学习的强假设，实现了更实际的TBSG学习框架；引入的博弈论ECD参数为可达性目标的学习复杂度提供了有效度量。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [143] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对小型精选数据集进行微调会导致语言模型在未训练过的无关领域产生意识形态泛化，且该效应在多个模型、数据集和评估方式上稳定存在，同时保持通用能力基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对窄领域、事实可辩护、通过审核的数据集进行微调，是否会导致语言模型在无关领域产生广泛的意识形态偏移，以及该偏移的幅度和机制。

Method: 在GPT-4.1和Gemma-3上使用左右倾向的经济学问答、HR政策、金融查询等数据集进行微调，测量模型在刑事司法、环境、文化品味等无关话题上的立场变化；提出广度（breadth）和放大（amplification）两个指标量化泛化效应，并与少样本提示进行对比。

Result: 微调导致模型在未训练领域产生匹配方向的意识形态偏移，且偏移幅度超出少样本提示；在极端情况下产生远超出分布的输出（如认可种族-IQ关联和政治暴力）；效应在混合通用数据后仍存在，GSM8K精度变化在±1pp以内。

Conclusion: 微调在窄领域数据上会引起广泛的意识形态泛化，且放大效应强于少样本提示；这种效应具有鲁棒性，对模型安全部署构成潜在风险。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>


### [144] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于真值表逻辑结构的二分类器最优线性组合方法。通过分类器对数据集的等价类划分，利用分类校准函数的多维推广对凸化经验风险进行严格分析。建立了存在唯一全局最小点的充分条件，针对三个分类器的情形列出了所有配置（唯一解、下确界或多解）。推导了指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的显式解析公式，并引入φ-前沿概念评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类器集成方法多依赖迭代优化求权重，缺乏对凸化经验风险全局最小点存在性与唯一性的严格理论分析，尤其在分类器数量较多时可能出现无最小点的情况，亟需建立系统的理论框架。

Method: 基于真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，通过等价类划分实现分类校准函数的多维推广；建立凸化经验风险全局最小点存在唯一的充分条件；对三个分类器的情形进行穷举配置分析；推导指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的显式解析公式；引入φ-前沿概念评估分类器稳定性与数据质量。

Result: 建立了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险全局最小点存在唯一的充分条件；完整列出了三个分类器的所有配置类型（唯一解、下确界、非唯一最小点）；获得了指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的解析公式；提出了φ-前沿用于稳定性与数据质量分析。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种无需迭代优化的分类器线性组合理论框架，在保证全局最优性的同时给出了显式解，并通过φ-前沿为分类器集成提供了可解释的稳定性评估工具。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [145] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本方法，通过在RML上进行指令微调后与原始推理模型合并，利用已有的监督微调数据（包含人工编写的解决方案）来提升推理语言模型（RLM）在可验证和难以验证领域的性能，成本不到3美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏可靠验证器的领域（如文本摘要）训练RLM具有挑战性，同时大量包含人工编写解决方案的监督微调数据未被有效利用。

Method: 首先对RLM进行经典的指令微调（不含推理轨迹），然后将指令微调后的模型与原始推理模型进行合并，以恢复其在目标领域的推理行为。

Result: 该方法在可验证领域（如编码）和难以验证领域（如文本摘要）均提升了RLM性能，同时保持了在其他领域的能力，且成本极低（不到3美元）。

Conclusion: 利用已有的监督微调数据通过指令微调+模型合并的方式可以有效提升RLM在多种领域的性能，是一种高度经济有效的方案。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [146] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机对数缩放（RLS）——一种即插即用的后处理防御方法，通过输出经随机缩放的伪造对数来混淆黑盒分数攻击，在保持模型准确率的同时显著降低攻击成功率；同时，针对现有非随机化防御AAA提出了自适应攻击，揭示了其脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本严重威胁机器学习模型的安全部署，现有黑盒攻击方法高效且查询成本低，亟需可扩展、低成本且强大的防御机制。

Method: 提出RLS（随机对数缩放）防御：在模型输出对数上应用随机缩放，生成伪造的分数以迷惑攻击者；同时设计针对AAA（一种非随机化对数修改防御）的自适应攻击。

Result: RLS显著降低了最先进黑盒分数攻击的成功率，同时保持模型准确率，且相比其他随机化防御方法具有更小的置信度分数失真；自适应攻击成功破解了AAA防御。

Conclusion: RLS是一种即插即用、轻量级的有效黑盒防御方案；非随机化黑盒防御在面对自适应攻击时存在脆弱性。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [147] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过逐步简化SOTA模型DynaMix，提出极简的兩参数模型DynaBase，该模型仅通过线性混合当前隐状态与最近上下文邻居及其时序后继即可实现优异的零样本动力系统重构，其参数数量比其他基础模型低多个数量级，并允许闭式解析求解和直接优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前零样本动力系统重构的基础模型虽泛化能力强，但内部机制不透明。理解这些机制有助于剥离冗余架构，揭示动力系统领域内上下文学习的最小需求。

Method: 从DynaMix出发逐步约简至两参数形式DynaBase，其预测为当前隐状态与最近上下文邻居及其时序后继的线性混合。进一步推导出单参数映射族，一端为上下文模仿算法，另一端为混沌行为。

Result: DynaBase在混沌和周期系统的零样本重构上极具竞争力，参数负载极低；支持对重构度量的直接优化及MSE的闭式一步解析解；不同训练策略分别优化短期预测或动力系统重构。

Conclusion: DynaBase揭示了零样本动力系统重构的最小机制，并在统一的数学框架内调和了文献中看似矛盾的观察结果。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>


### [148] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在序列化观测数据中，存在结果干扰的因果推断问题，提出了基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的高效方法，用于学习具有伊辛模型依赖性和低秩因子结构潜变量的模型参数，并在理论和非理论层面验证了该方法在因果效应估计上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当观测数据中存在时间序列上的结果干扰（即单元间的结果相互依赖）时，传统因果推断方法失效。本文旨在解决这种复杂依赖结构下的因果效应估计问题。

Method: 采用马尔可夫时序模型，将N个单元的二元结果建模为伊辛模型，引入外部场刻画处理效应和潜变量影响；潜变量建模为低秩因子结构；利用最大伪似然估计（MPLE）进行参数学习，并从学习模型中采样以估计因果量。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了参数估计的非渐近一致性，证明了该一致性可传递至因果量的可靠估计；通过合成实验和真实的美国县级疫苗接种率对COVID-19死亡率影响案例研究验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: MPLE方法能够高效处理存在结果干扰的序列化观测数据中的因果推断问题，理论保证与实证结果均表明该方法具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [149] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongStraw是一个面向百万级token强化学习后训练的架构感知执行栈，通过共享提示评估分离自动求导、仅保留模型关键状态、逐条回放短响应分支等策略，在固定GPU预算下显著扩展了可训练上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理上下文长度已接近百万token，而RL后训练通常仅支持256K token以内，导致部署时依赖长度泛化，尤其阻碍AI agent在长轨迹场景下的训练效果。

Method: 采用GRPO算法，共享提示部分不参与自动求导，仅保留后续token所需的模型状态；短响应分支逐条回放以减少实时训练图规模，以额外回放时间换取内存节省。

Result: 在8块H20 GPU上，分组Qwen评分与响应反向传播达2.1M位置；分组大小从2增至8仅增加0.21 GB峰值内存；压力测试达4.46M位置。32块H20 GPU上验证了GLM-5.2全78层2.1M token提示的端到端执行。

Conclusion: LongStraw建立了百万token级RL后训练的执行能力，但提示状态为分离态且部分分布式前向与梯度组合路径未完整实现，尚需验证完整训练正确性。

Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

</details>


### [150] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对文本到图像模型生成多样性不足及人口统计偏差问题，提出多轴max@K——一种基于组的强化学习目标，通过跨样本的最大值信用分配机制提升预定义语义模式覆盖度，在保持图像质量与文本对齐的同时显著提升公平性评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型对同一提示词生成的图像视觉模式单一、多样性有限，且在人相关的提示词上会反映或放大人口统计偏差，缺乏对预定义语义模式覆盖的正式定义与优化方法。

Method: 提出多轴max@K目标：对一组样本，先取每个类别上所有样本的最大得分，再对各类别最大值求和；信用分配仅在样本提升某类别的组内最大值时赋予正权重，使不同样本可贡献于不同类别。该目标用于扩散T2I模型的强化学习微调。

Result: 在合成混合数据及SD3.5-M上的确定性颜色奖励验证了信用分配机制的有效性；在感知外观公平性评估中，对三个自动评估器在未见提示词上，多轴max@K将公平性评分提升0.23-0.36（相对基础模型），同时保持了图像质量与文本对齐。

Conclusion: 多轴max@K能有效提升T2I模型对多种语义模式的覆盖，改善生成多样性并缓解人口统计偏差，且不牺牲生成质量与提示对齐性能。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [151] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态对比学习中医学数据存在语义相似的假阴性样本问题，提出MseaCL框架，通过融入影像报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，在儿科3D脑MRI和放射学报告上预训练，在下游任务（如儿童脑肿瘤分子分类）中实现AUC至少提升22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习假设批次内非配对样本均为负样本，但在医学场景中，样本间常共享高层语义属性，导致假阴性样本破坏表征质量。

Method: 提出MseaCL框架，在对比学习过程中引入放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，以缓解语义相似的假阴性样本对表征学习的负面影响。

Result: 在儿科3D脑MRI和放射学报告上预训练后，下游儿科脑肿瘤分子分类任务的AUC至少提升22.6%。

Conclusion: MseaCL能够有效缓解医学多模态对比学习中的假阴性问题，实现更鲁棒、语义对齐的多模态表征，在临床应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [152] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新估计器，用于在contextual bandits任务中仅使用离线数据进行离策略评估。该估计器结合了加权重要性采样的有界性和普通重要性采样的线性特性，被证明具有渐近一致性，并在复杂条件下（包括行为策略误设定）的经验表现优于强基线方法（包括普通加权重要性采样）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离策略评估方法（如普通重要性采样和加权重要性采样）各自存在局限性：重要性采样方差大且无界，加权重要性采样虽具有有界性但存在偏差。作者希望设计一种能结合两者优势的新估计器，在保持有界性的同时减少偏差，从而在复杂条件下（如行为策略误设定）实现更可靠的评估。

Method: Kernel-WIS估计器通过核函数对重要性采样权重进行平滑处理，融合了普通重要性采样的线性特性和加权重要性采样的有界性。具体地，利用核方法对权重分布进行非参数估计，在保持估计量有界的同时引入线性结构，从而降低方差并改善行为策略误设定下的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析表明Kernel-WIS具有渐近一致性。在实验中，该方法在多种复杂条件下（包括行为策略误设定）显著优于普通重要性采样和加权重要性采样等强基线方法，表现出更强的鲁棒性和更低的估计误差。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS通过巧妙的核化设计成功融合了重要性采样和加权重要性采样的互补优势，为离线离策略评估提供了一种理论上可靠、实践中有竞争力的新方案，特别适用于行为策略可能存在误设定的实际应用场景。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [153] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SINDy方法及其工程应用扩展，包括噪声鲁棒的弱形式、集成学习变体以及约束和参数化公式，并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个案例展示了该方法在工程中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程问题中控制方程通常未知或难以表征，而神经网络等代理模型需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性。SINDy方法通过稀疏回归从较小的数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，但其在工程问题中的应用尚未得到充分记录，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文采用三部曲结构：第一部分介绍标准SINDy算法并逐步扩展，包括噪声鲁棒的弱形式SINDy、基于集成学习的变体以及带约束和参数化的SINDy公式；第二部分和第三部分分别通过无人机（UAV）系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个详细案例研究进行方法验证。

Result: 通过无人机系统辨识案例和混沌热虹吸换热器案例，证明了SINDy方法实现简单、灵活性强，能够作为高级工程应用的有价值的辨识工具。

Conclusion: SINDy方法简单易实现且具备足够的灵活性，能够有效应用于高级工程场景中的系统辨识问题，为工程领域的模型发现提供了一种兼具数据效率和物理可解释性的替代方案。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [154] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了协变量平衡诊断在离线强化学习（RL）应用于最优治疗方案推荐时，检测隐藏混杂/模型误设的有效性。结果表明，现有离线RL研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究。无论如何，现有离线RL研究无法被认定为统计稳健。结论提出了未来研究方向，以推动离线RL在治疗方案推荐问题中的方法论更稳健的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL在治疗方案推荐中的应用日益增多，但隐藏混杂和模型误设可能导致偏倚。现有研究缺乏对其统计稳健性的系统评估，本文旨在探索协变量平衡诊断方法在此类场景下的检测能力。

Method: 采用协变量平衡诊断方法，对现有离线RL治疗方案推荐研究进行系统性审查和评估，分析隐藏混杂/模型误设的存在性及协变量平衡指标的适用性。

Result: 发现现有离线RL研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以有效评估这些研究；无论哪种情况，现有研究均无法被认定为统计稳健。

Conclusion: 当前离线RL在治疗方案推荐中的应用缺乏统计稳健性，未来需要发展更完善的评估方法和协变量平衡诊断工具，以提高研究的方法学可靠性。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [155] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LINCS分类框架，通过微分非组合性的修复来解决机器学习中的组合性问题。该框架将机器学习问题建模为sketch（带有交换性条件、极限锥和余极限余锥的图），利用切线结构研究无穷小扰动是否保持组合性约束，并通过INC余代数的最终不动点形式化机器学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法通常依赖损失函数的标量化或向量空间假设，缺乏对组合性（compositionality）的形式化处理。作者试图建立一个纯粹的范畴论框架，将非组合性定义为通用分解问题的失败，而非预测值与真实值之间的算术误差。

Method: (1) 将机器学习问题规范化为sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$；(2) 定义基础缺陷 $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$ 作为因子分解的障碍；(3) 引入切向量提升 $T$，得到LINCS为 $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$；(4) 定义INC内函子，构建$D,TD,T^2D,\cdots$的因子分解问题塔；(5) 利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 建立了LINCS框架，将机器学习形式化为对余代数不动点 $\nu T_{\mbox{INC}}$ 的搜索，并在特定条件下证明了最终INC余代数的存在性。实验评估正在进行中。

Conclusion: LINCS为机器学习提供了一个纯粹的范畴论形式化框架，将组合性修复作为核心机制，为理解深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习中的学习过程提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [156] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)的新型强化学习蒸馏方法，通过定义教师模型与其基础模型在推理指令微调前后的差异（delta信号）作为蒸馏奖励，取代直接模仿教师输出分布的方式，在数学、科学和代码推理基准上显著优于传统蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于策略蒸馏方法虽然缓解了奖励模型的约束，但其基本设计尚未得到充分探索。直接模仿教师输出分布的方式效率有限，需要更直接的信号来传递推理能力。

Method: 提出delta信号，定义为教师模型与其未经过推理指令微调的基础模型之间的差异，捕捉推理微调带来的变化。基于此信号设计蒸馏奖励，替代直接模仿教师输出分布，形成On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准上的实验表明，OPD²一致性地优于传统基于策略蒸馏方法，仅需短期的后训练即可使推理LLM达到强劲性能。

Conclusion: OPD²通过引入delta信号作为蒸馏奖励，更有效地传递了推理能力，为基于策略的蒸馏方法提供了一种更优的设计范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [157] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合神经-物理框架，将已知ODE分量保留为显式形式，用神经网络表示缺失分量，通过RTS平滑器和反向传播交替估计潜在状态和模型参数，在部分状态观测下学习缺失的ODE组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力系统中部分动力学方程未知，且仅有部分状态变量可被测量，现有方法难以同时利用已知物理结构和处理不完全观测数据。

Method: 两阶段交替迭代：第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，使用RTS平滑器从观测数据推断潜在状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上，该方法能从部分观测中学习缺失的ODE组件，提升潜在状态重建和长期预测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能够利用并保持可解释的机械结构，在不完全测量条件下有效学习缺失的动力学方程。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [158] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，针对世界-动作模型（WAM）引入一类新的对抗攻击——世界-动作漂移攻击，通过微小视觉扰动破坏模型想象与执行之间的对齐。该框架包含两种攻击变体：纯动作攻击（直接导致任务失败）和想象保持攻击（保持未来预测不变但诱导有害动作偏移）。实验表明攻击能显著降低任务成功率（如从96.5%降至43.1%），且想象保持攻击揭示了WAM特有的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管WAM通过耦合动作生成与未来世界预测被认为具有鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性，但这种耦合本身可能成为攻击面。作者旨在揭示这一假设的脆弱性，并系统性建模和评估针对WAM的对抗攻击。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，定义了两类攻击：(1) 动作仅对抗攻击——直接驱动模型执行任务失败的动作；(2) 想象保持对抗攻击——在保持模型预测的未来接近干净想象的同时，诱导有害动作偏移。攻击沿两个维度（攻击强度与隐蔽性）进行刻画，并在不同WAM变体上进行评估。

Result: 动作仅攻击将任务成功率从96.5%降至43.1%；想象保持攻击在保持未来预测漂移较小的条件下仍能保持强攻击性能，暴露出WAM特有的脆弱性。

Conclusion: WAM的想象与执行之间的对齐是可被攻破的，存在世界-动作漂移攻击的实际威胁；即便模型看起来预测了合理的未来，其实际动作仍可能被恶意偏移，这对WAM的安全部署提出警示。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [159] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对独立同质机器的块替换策略，提出了数据驱动的多臂老虎机算法，在寿命分布未知时学习成本最优的替换间隔k*，实现了O(K log T)的遗憾界，并通过嵌套观测性质进一步降低遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际运维中机器寿命分布未知，传统块替换策略依赖分布假设，需要从带删失的运行数据中自适应学习最优替换周期以最小化长期平均成本。

Method: 将问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出基于Hoeffding和Bernstein的置信下界（LCB）算法；利用块替换的嵌套观测性质设计相关变体；提出Kaplan-Meier更新非参数估计算法处理删失数据；建立两种平均成本MDP模型（时间流逝和年龄向量）进行理论分析。

Result: Hoeffding/Bernstein LCB算法达到O(K log T)遗憾（匹配Lai-Robbins下界）；相关变体达到O((K-k*)log T)遗憾，仅需O(1)次直接拉动次优臂k<k*；Kaplan-Meier更新算法实现几乎必然策略一致性和长期近零增量遗憾。

Conclusion: 证明了块替换在给定策略类中对任意寿命分布的最优性，并揭示了递增失效率分布下年龄向量MDP的单调阈值结构。数值实验验证了理论排序并显示了块替换与年龄相关替换之间的结构性成本差距。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [160] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KP-tree的可变草图（Mutable Sketches）方法，通过在用户评分到达时即时重算嵌入向量，解决了二阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧性问题。在KuaiRec数据集上，仅用1.8%的数据读取就达到了0.810的RMSE（ALS需100%数据，0.822），且新用户首条评分后可在1ms内获得个性化推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶段推荐系统中，用户对项目评分后其嵌入向量保持不变直至下一训练周期，导致嵌入陈旧性（embedding staleness）问题，影响推荐的实时性和准确性。

Method: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches），采用KP-tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好，仅需拟合一次低秩投影，评分到达时即可即时重算嵌入向量。KP-tree采用基于范数的比例采样策略处理稀疏数据。

Result: KuaiRec数据集上，可变草图在仅读取1.8%数据时达到0.810 RMSE，优于ALS在100%数据下的0.822；每批次更新速度快8倍；新用户首次评分后可在1ms内获得个性化推荐；在稀疏数据（<1%密度）下，范数比例采样的物品覆盖率比均匀采样高40-130%。

Conclusion: 可变草图方法有效解决了嵌入陈旧性问题，实现了接近最优的预测精度，同时大幅降低了计算开销和存储需求，并能即时响应新评分和新用户，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [161] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体推文的综合方法，用于分析比特币市场情绪，而非预测价格。通过融合情绪趋势与链上及金融指标，并使用XGBoost模型进行情绪分类，平均F1分数达0.84。SHAP方法用于解释链上特征对模型预测的贡献，提高了模型透明度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为一种去中心化数字资产和投资工具的使用日益增长，激发了理解其市场行为的强烈兴趣。现有研究多关注价格预测，而本文旨在解释市场情绪，填补了结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体数据综合分析比特币市场情绪的研究空白。

Method: 1) 数据融合：将区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据与每日推特情绪分类结果相结合；2) 数据标准化：将情绪趋势与链上及金融指标归一化为统一的标准化数据集；3) 模型训练与评估：使用交叉验证测试多种机器学习模型；4) 模型选择：Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) 被确定为最可靠的分类模型；5) 可解释性分析：采用基于博弈论的SHAP方法量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献。

Result: XGBoost模型在情绪分类任务中取得了约0.84的平均F1分数，验证了数据组合能够产生有意义的预测信号。SHAP分析揭示了链上特征的具体贡献，提升了模型透明度。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据组合方法能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析，并为未来引入深度学习进行模型改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Deep-learning Causal Retrieval Optimization for Efficient e-commerce Distribution in Pinterest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14161)
*Junpeng Hou,XianXing Zhang,Sai Xiao,Derek Cheng,Darren Reger,Olafur Gudmundsson,Mehdi Ben Ayed,Zhiqing Rao,Huizhong Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Pinterest早期检索阶段学习个性化、上下文感知的购物触发策略的因果决策方法，通过深度多任务模型联合预测多个事件的结果和提升度，采用双重稳健伪结果与标定结果损失相结合的方式，实现稳定且单一稳健的提升学习。线性时间离线回放选择阈值并预测策略影响，与在线结果高度一致。生产化部署后，在保持关键购物会话中性不变的情况下，购物触发量减少多达85%，总会话数提升+0.26%，Pin保存提升+1.10%，并带来显著的基础设施节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Pinterest平台上，用户浏览灵感并转向行动（如发现可购物内容），需要在不分散用户注意力的情况下分发商业内容。现有方法难以在早期检索阶段做出准确的触发决策，因此需要一种因果决策框架，通过学习个性化与上下文感知的触发策略，平衡用户意图、探索成本与系统效率。

Method: 将购物候选生成器的触发决策建模为因果问题；部署深度多任务模型联合预测多个事件的结果和提升度；使用双重稳健（Doubly-Robust）伪目标与标定结果损失相结合，实现单一稳健的提升学习；通过随机数据日志提供反事实覆盖；设计线性时间离线回放方法选择阈值并预测策略影响；生产化部署中实现与远程检索调用的并行运行，无端到端延迟回归。

Result: 购物触发量减少高达85%；关键购物会话保持中性；总会话数提升+0.26%；Pin保存提升+1.10%；带来显著的基础设施节省；离线回放预测与在线结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种通用的、实用的早期检索优化方案，可适用于现代级联推荐系统中购物以外的场景，实现了探索成本与用户意图在规模上的对齐。

Abstract: Pinterest is where people turn inspiration into action as users browse ideas, then take steps toward realization, often by discovering shoppable content. To support this journey, we must distribute commerce content when it helps, not when it distracts. We frame this as a causal decision of triggering shopping candidate generators in early retrieval and deploy a production system at Pinterest that learns personalized and contextualized triggering policies. A deep multi-task model jointly predicts outcomes and uplift of multiple events, trained with a doubly-robust pseudo-outcome alongside calibrated outcome losses for stable, single-robust uplift learning. A randomized data logging supplies counterfactual coverage, and the model is evaluated by both regular and reverse metrics for full assessment. A linear-time offline replay is designed to select thresholds and forecast policy impact with extremely high consistency with online results. For productionization, the model runs in parallel with remote retrieval calls without end-to-end latency regression. At web scale, we cut shopping triggers by up to 85% while holding key shopping sessions neutral, improving important total sessions (+0.26%) and Pin saves (+1.10%), with significant infrastructure savings. By unifying deep causal learning with reliable offline replay and demonstrating production-grade deployment, this work provides a generally practical recipe for early-retrieval optimizations in modern cascading recommenders beyond shopping, aligning exploration and cost with user intent at scale.

</details>


### [163] [Long-History User Transformers for Real-Time Ad Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14331)
*Viacheslav Ovchinnikov,Georgii Smirnov,Nikolai Savushkin,Veronika Ivanova,Maksim Kuzin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段广告CTR预测架构：离线用高容量Transformer异步编码用户长序列历史，将结果缓存用于在线轻量级模型推理，在严格延迟约束下实现了显著的指标提升（搜索广告+2.77%，展示广告+2.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告CTR预测面临长交互序列信息与严格服务延迟（毫秒级）之间的根本矛盾，现有方案要么丢弃长历史信息，要么因计算开销过大无法部署。

Method: 解耦历史编码与实时推理：离线Transformer以自回归方式预训练（双重目标：反馈预测+下一项预测），将用户全跨面交互历史压缩为紧凑表示并缓存；在线轻量模型结合缓存表示、最近事件和请求上下文进行CTR预测，两阶段联合微调。

Result: 离线实验恢复全历史在线Transformer 72-80%的效果；在线A/B测试中搜索广告排名指标+2.77%，广告网络+2.1%，收入分别增长+2.26%和+0.43%，且未增加服务延迟。

Conclusion: 解耦式离线-在线架构是解决长序列建模与实时推理冲突的有效生产方案，缓存表示对陈旧性具有鲁棒性，支持低成本刷新策略。

Abstract: Long interaction histories are among the most informative inputs for click-through rate (CTR) prediction, yet in online advertising they collide with a hard serving constraint: ads must be scored within a few hundred milliseconds to enter the auction, which rules out running a large sequence encoder at request time. We describe how a production advertising system resolves this conflict by decoupling history encoding from real-time inference. A high-capacity offline transformer asynchronously encodes the user's full cross-surface interaction history into a compact representation cached in a feature store, while a lightweight runtime model combines this cached representation with the user's most recent events and the request context at serving time. The offline encoder is pre-trained autoregressively on large-scale interaction logs with a dual objective - feedback prediction and next-item prediction - and the two-stage architecture is then fine-tuned for CTR prediction on the target advertising surface. Offline, the split design recovers 72-80% of the quality of a full-history runtime transformer that would be too expensive to deploy, and the cached representation is robust enough to staleness to permit inexpensive refresh policies. In production A/B experiments, the system improves the primary ranking metric by +2.77% in search advertising and +2.1% on the Yandex Advertising Network, with revenue gains of +2.26% and +0.43% respectively - without increasing serving latency.

</details>


### [164] [Impact of Expert-Following Strategies in Financial Asset Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14556)
*Ryuki Unno,Koshi Watanabe,Keigo Sakurai,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出专家跟随策略（Expert-Following Strategies），通过识别历史上高ROI的投资者并推荐其购买的资产（按ROI加权购买频率打分），在真实交易数据上实现了收益率（ROI）和相关性（nDCG）的双重显著提升，解决了传统单目标优化策略在盈利性和偏好对齐之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融交易推荐中，基于收益和基于偏好的策略各自只优化单一目标，导致盈利性（ROI）与相关性（nDCG）之间存在根本性权衡，缺乏能同时兼顾两者的方法。

Method: 提出专家跟随策略框架：根据历史ROI识别表现最好的投资者，推荐他们购买的资产，并采用ROI加权购买频率对推荐资产进行打分排序。

Result: 在真实交易历史数据上的实验表明，该策略在所有四个阈值下均能在ROI和nDCG两个指标上同时取得相比市场平均基线具有统计显著性的提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的专家跟随策略能够有效突破传统单目标优化方法的局限性，在金融推荐中实现盈利性和用户偏好对齐的双赢。

Abstract: Financial institutions hold rich transaction histories, yet delivering recommendations that simultaneously maximize investment returns and ensure preference alignment remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches, namely return-based and preference-based strategies, each optimize a single objective, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between profitability (ROI) and relevance (nDCG). In this paper, we propose the Expert-Following Strategies: a framework that identifies top-performing investors based on their historical ROI and recommends the assets they purchased, scored by ROI-weighted purchase frequency. Our experiments using real-world transaction histories show that our strategy achieves statistically significant improvement over the market-average baseline in both ROI and nDCG simultaneously across all four thresholds.

</details>


### [165] [LLM-Based Re-Ranking for Real Estate Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14835)
*Nkateko Ntimane,Rafel Guedes,Tiago Cunha,Pedro Nogueira*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QuintoAndar Group提出了基于大语言模型的重排序器，用于增强对话式房源推荐系统。该重排序器通过整合用户多轮对话中的上下文意图来重新排序候选房源。研究构建了包含96万查询-房源对的大规模离线评估数据集，采用LLM-as-a-Judge框架进行标注并辅以人工验证。离线评估和在线A/B测试均显示排序质量持续提升，生产环境中点击率提升5.3%，预约看房量提升4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统房源搜索依赖结构化过滤器和分面搜索，难以捕捉用户在多轮对话中表达的复杂、多维且依赖于上下文的租房/购房意图。随着对话式助手的普及，用户期望通过开放式多轮对话表达需求，现有系统无法满足这种交互方式的排序需求。

Method: 提出基于LLM的重排序器，通过理解用户在对话历史中表达的细微、上下文丰富的意图，对初始检索到的候选房源进行重新排序。同时构建了包含96万查询-房源对的大规模离线评估数据集，采用LLM-as-a-Judge框架进行自动标注并辅以人工验证，用于训练和评估。

Result: 离线评估和在线生产环境A/B测试均显示排序质量提升：生产环境中点击率提升5.3%，预约看房量提升4.8%，且具有统计显著性。验证了将对话上下文融入房源推荐的有效性。

Conclusion: 将对话上下文整合到房源推荐系统中能够显著提升排序质量和用户体验，基于LLM的重排序器是应对复杂对话式搜索需求的有效方案。

Abstract: QuintoAndar Group operates the leading housing marketplace in Latin America for both rentals and sales. The platform replaces traditionally paper-heavy workflows with a fully digital experience, making housing transactions faster and more accessible to tenants, buyers, and landlords in the region. Finding the ideal home in such a vast catalog is inherently difficult. At the same time, the widespread adoption of conversational assistants is reshaping user expectations: people increasingly want to express their needs through open, multi-turn dialog rather than rigid filter menus and faceted search. This shift is particularly pronounced in housing, where intent is multi-dimensional, context-dependent, and rarely reducible to a small set of structured constraints. To meet these expectations, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM) based re-ranker that augments a conversational recommendation system by reordering retrieved candidates according to the nuanced, context-rich intent expressed across the user's conversation. We additionally construct a large-scale offline evaluation dataset for conversational real-estate search, containing 960,000 query-item pairs constructed from both synthetic and production queries and annotated using an LLM-as-a-Judge framework with human validation. We validate our approach both offline, on this proprietary dataset, and online, through a production A/B test. Both evaluations show consistent improvements in ranking quality, including a statistically significant increase in production of +5.3% in click-through rate and +4.8% in scheduled visits, demonstrating the value of integrating conversational context into housing recommendations.

</details>


### [166] [Does generative AI supersede supervised XMLC? A Benchmark Study on Automated Subject Indexing with German Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14882)
*Maximilian Kähler,Katja Konermann,Lisa Kluge,Markus Schumacher*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究将图书馆自动主题标引视为极端多标签分类问题，对比了多种监督式XMLC方法、词汇匹配基线和三种基于LLM的生成方法在德国国家图书馆德文科学文献上的表现。结果显示，基于Transformer密集特征的XMLC方法在整体二元相关性指标上最优，但LLM生成方法在分级相关性和长尾词汇性能上更优，是未来生产应用的有前景替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆主题标引依赖大型受控词表，传统方法难以处理大规模标签集，需要探索极端多标签分类和LLM等新方法在此任务上的效果。

Method: 应用多种监督式XMLC方法、词汇匹配基线方法以及三种基于LLM的生成方法，在德国国家图书馆当代德语科学文献数据集上进行对比实验，采用二元相关性指标（与已有标引对比）和分级相关性评估（由专业馆员评定）进行评价。

Result: 基于Transformer密集特征的监督式XMLC算法在整体二元相关性指标上表现最佳；LLM生成方法在分级相关性和主题词表长尾部分的推荐性能上更优。

Conclusion: LLM生成方法虽在整体二元指标上不及最佳XMLC方法，但在分级相关性和长尾词推荐方面表现更好，是未来图书馆主题标引生产应用中有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: With a large controlled vocabulary as the label set, the task of automated subject indexing in a library can be understood as a multi-label classification task. If the set of subject terms is large, the problem fits the Extreme Multi-Label Classification (XMLC) objective. In this study, we apply a selection of specialised supervised XMLC methods to the test case of subject indexing contemporary German scientific literature, collected at the German National Library (DNB). We contrast these results by including a classical lexical matching baseline and three of our own recently developed LLM-based methods into the benchmark. Algorithms are evaluated and compared in several metrics. This includes binary relevance comparisons with previously indexed material, as well as graded relevance ratings by professional subject librarians. A challenge for all methods is to reliably make suggestions from the long tail of the subject vocabulary. We find that supervised XMLC algorithms relying on transformer-based dense features give best results in terms of overall binary relevance metrics. However, focusing on graded relevance and performance in the long tail of our subject vocabulary, the LLM-based generative methods give better results, making them a promising alternative for future productive use.

</details>


### [167] [CoSimRec: Measuring Coordinated-Content Penetration in Recommender Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15114)
*Nan Li,Jiahong Shao,Jiuyang Lyu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoSimRec离线评估框架及APR指标族，用于测量推荐系统中协调行为对非机器人用户曝光和参与度的渗透影响。在三个数据集上实验表明，流行度驱动和反馈敏感的排序算法会显著放大协调活动的渗透率，而同步感知排序可有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统鲁棒性评估仅关注静态目标排名变化，未能捕捉协调交互、推荐和用户响应在反馈循环中的动态演化，缺乏测量协调行为对非机器人用户实际渗透效果的指标。

Method: 提出CoSimRec离线基于智能体的评估框架，建模协调账号、动态排序、非机器人响应和排序干预的闭环过程；引入APR指标族（算法渗透率）衡量目标内容在非机器人曝光和参与中的占比、相对于无攻击基线的提升以及每次协调交互获得的曝光增量。

Result: 在MIND、MovieLens、LastFM上使用随机、流行度、反馈敏感、MF和BPR-MF推荐器实验。随机控制无统计显著正渗透；流行度和反馈敏感排序在所有六个主从数据集-推荐器设置中产生显著正APR-Lift（LastFM上达0.4505）；同步感知排序在每种对应防御设置中均降低APR。

Conclusion: 协调活动在流行度驱动和反馈敏感的推荐系统中被显著放大，同步感知排序可作为有效防御手段。

Abstract: Recommender systems increasingly shape which content reaches users, making it important to understand whether coordinated activity is amplified beyond the accounts that initiate it. Existing robustness evaluations largely focus on static target-rank changes and do not capture how coordinated interactions, recommendation, and user response evolve within a feedback loop. To address this gap, we propose CoSimRec, an offline agent-based evaluation framework that models coordinated accounts, dynamic ranking, non-bot responses, and ranking interventions in a shared closed-loop process. CoSimRec introduces the Algorithmic Penetration Rate (APR) metric family to measure target content's share of non-bot exposure and engagement, lift against matched no-attack baselines, and exposure gained per coordinated interaction. We evaluate CoSimRec on MIND, MovieLens, and LastFM using random, popularity-based, feedback-sensitive, MF, and BPR-MF recommenders, with ten-seed inference for the primary APR analysis and population-scale experiments of up to 1000 users. Random controls show no statistically supported positive penetration, whereas popularity-based and feedback-sensitive ranking produce significant positive APR-Lift in all six master-worker dataset--recommender settings, reaching 0.4505 on LastFM; synchronization-aware ranking reduces APR in every corresponding defense setting.

</details>


### [168] [Bridge Evidence: Static Retrieval Utility Does Not Predict Causal Utility in Multi-Step Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15253)
*Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发现并量化了静态检索相关性与智能体检索中因果有用性之间的差距。通过反事实轨迹效用（CTU）评估，约三分之一的文档在静态评估中看似无用，但对智能体后续搜索至关重要（桥接文档）。原因在于这些文档提供了区分性实体，帮助智能体重新定向搜索。静态相关性与因果有用性在智能体检索中几乎统计独立（Spearman rho = -0.026）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索系统基于静态有用性（文档对当前问题的答案提升）进行训练和评估，但这种方法在语言模型作为搜索智能体、多轮推理查询时失效——文档的价值可能体现在它让智能体下一步能做什么，而非对当前问题的直接贡献。作者旨在量化这一差距。

Method: 基于ReAct风格智能体在HotpotQA数据集上回放1000个开发问题，对智能体读取的每个文档执行反事实删除并重新运行轨迹，计算CTU（综合最终答案质量、下一轮检索质量、轮次数的变化）。将CTU与静态RAG效用（SRU）进行交叉对比（23,322个文档观测）。此外，使用可观测实体相关性（OER）分析桥接文档的作用机制。

Result: CTU与SRU接近统计独立（Spearman rho = -0.026）。约三分之一的文档被智能体读取并起到因果支撑作用，但在静态读者看来无用（桥接文档）。改用BM25和交叉编码器代理后，桥接单元仍占27.2%。桥接文档中的判别性实体出现在智能体下一轮查询中的频率是非相关文档中实体的4.02倍（6.1% vs 1.5%）。

Conclusion: 静态相关性和因果有用性在智能体检索中是不同的量，优化前者并不能带来后者。桥接文档通过提供判别性实体来重定向搜索，从而发挥价值。

Abstract: Retrieval systems are trained and evaluated on a static idea of usefulness: hand a document and a question to a reader model, see whether the answer improves, and score the document accordingly. The idea holds up when a document is read on its own. It breaks when a language model works as a search agent, issuing several queries and reasoning across turns, because a document can matter for what it lets the agent do next rather than for what it says about the current question.
  We measure that gap rather than argue it. Using a ReAct style agent over HotpotQA, we replay 1000 development questions and, for every document the agent read, delete it and re-run the rest of the trajectory from that point. Comparing the original run against its counterfactual gives a Counterfactual Trajectory Utility (CTU) score from three deltas: final answer quality, next query retrieval quality, and turn count. Crossing CTU against Static RAG Utility (SRU) over 23,322 document observations, the two are close to statistically independent (Spearman rho = -0.026). Roughly a third of the documents the agent reads are causally load bearing while looking useless to a static reader; we call these bridge documents. The pattern survives when the reader based axis is swapped for a BM25 and cross encoder proxy, giving a bridge cell of 27.2% on an evenly spread axis.
  A second experiment pins down the mechanism. Using the Observable Entity Relevance (OER) measure from prior work, entities that discriminate relevant from non-relevant candidates appear in the agent's next query 4.02 times more often than entities found only in non-relevant documents (6.1% vs 1.5%, n = 227,139). A bridge document earns its keep by handing the agent a discriminative entity that redirects the search. Static relevance and causal usefulness are different quantities in agentic retrieval, and optimizing the first does not deliver the second.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Beyond Object Validation: Relational Conformance in Multi-Artifact Agent Releases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14155)
*Tengjiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了代理系统中最终发布包逃过审查的问题，提出了Schema-SIP关系一致性（SIP-RC）模型，将发布建模为图结构，包含声明与证据的对应、决策的有限权威、衍生工件的执行条件和谱系，以及发布字节与已检查包的一致性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理系统对输入、工具调用和生成对象进行了验证，但最终发布的包往往逃过了相同的审查，导致包内部自相矛盾。例如在一次DRSS发布中，账本支持60分和一个失败证书，但报告却宣布了100分的金牌路径，尽管每个本地门都是绿灯。

Method: 通过分析DRSS发布失败案例、Schema Docs（类似故障变成产品合同）和Brand Shuttle GEO（证据被转化为修复工作），提出了Schema-SIP关系一致性（SIP-RC）模型。该模型将发布建模为图：声明指向证据，决策携带有限权威，派生工件保留执行条件和谱系，发布的字节必须与已检查的包匹配。硬故障不能被平均掉，验证器在独立路径上重新计算关键决策。

Result: 建立了故障分类，并展示了若干机制是可行的。但完整配置文件是否优于现有检查仍是一个开放实验。

Conclusion: 识别了代理系统中发布包自相矛盾的故障类别，提出了SIP-RC作为候选解决方案，但其整体有效性有待进一步实验验证。

Abstract: Agent systems validate inputs, tool calls, and generated objects. The final package often escapes the same scrutiny. In one DRSS release, the ledger supported 60 points and a failed certificate; the report announced a 100-point Gold Path. Every local gate was green. The package contradicted itself.
  We study that failure alongside Schema Docs, where similar faults became product contracts, and Brand Shuttle GEO, where evidence is turned into repair work. The result is a candidate Schema-SIP Relational Conformance profile (SIP-RC). It models a release as a graph: claims point to evidence, decisions carry bounded authority, derived artifacts retain their execution conditions and lineage, and published bytes must match the package that was checked. Hard failures cannot be averaged away, and a validator recomputes critical decisions on a separate path. The paper establishes the failure class and shows that several mechanisms are practical. Whether the full profile performs better than existing checks remains an open experiment.

</details>


### [170] [Natural-Language to SysMLv2 Translation via Conformance-Driven Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14162)
*Chance LaVoie,Eladio Andujar Lugo,Taylan G. Topcu,Levent Burak Kara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符合性检查器驱动的自然语言到SysMLv2翻译框架，通过“生成-检查-修复”循环确保生成模型达到生产级可接受标准。在SysMBench的151个提示集上，单次生成的生产符合性接受率为51.16%，而该方法达到100%的符合率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着SysMLv2作为文本语言的标准化，将自然语言描述直接翻译为可执行模型的需求日益增长。然而，实际部署要求生成的模型能被工业建模环境接受，而不仅仅是满足语法约束。现有方法缺乏对生产级符合性的保证。

Method: 提出一个符合性检查器驱动框架，将SysMLv2符合性检查器嵌入到“生成-检查-修复”循环中。每次生成的模型由检查器评估，根据确定性诊断信息进行修订，直到零符合性错误。以生产级检查器作为验证标准，确保目标为可部署性而非仅语法合理性。

Result: 在SysMBench完整提示集（151个提示，4个LLM后端，共计604个提示-模型案例）上，单次生成的生产符合性接受率为51.16%，而所提出方法达到100%的符合率。

Conclusion: 将生产符合性从后处理检查提升为生成过程中的控制机制，该方法将概率性输出转化为生产可接受的SysMLv2工件，可直接用于加载、可视化和工程使用。

Abstract: Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) relies on formal system models as primary technical artifacts for representing requirements, structure, and behavior across the system lifecycle. With the standardization of SysMLv2 as a textual language, interest is increasing in translating natural-language descriptions directly into executable models. For practical deployment, generated models must be accepted by industrial modeling environments, not merely satisfy grammar constraints. We present a conformance-checker-driven framework for reliable natural-language-to-SysMLv2 translation that enforces production-level acceptance as the termination condition. The system embeds a SysMLv2 conformance checker within a generate-check-repair loop. Each model is evaluated using the checker, and deterministic diagnostics are incorporated into revisions until zero conformance errors are achieved. Using the production checker as the oracle ensures the framework targets deployability rather than grammar plausibility. We evaluate the approach on the full SysMBench prompt set of 151 prompts across four large language model backends, yielding 604 prompt-model cases. Single-shot generation achieves 51.16% production-conformance acceptance, while our approach achieves 100.00% conformance. By elevating production conformance from a post-processing check to a control mechanism within generation, the framework converts probabilistic outputs into production-accepted SysMLv2 artifacts suitable for loading, visualization, and engineering use.

</details>


### [171] [Towards Reliable AI-Assisted Analog Design: Template-Constrained LLM Agents for SAR ADC Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14165)
*Dimple Vijay Kochar,Hae-Seung Lee,Anantha P. Chandrakasan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架，一个基于LLM的多智能体端到端系统，通过专家知识约束和模板约束生成，成功生成能通过SPICE仿真的SAR ADC电路，解决了LLM在模拟EDA应用中因缺乏电路拓扑理解而产生的幻觉和失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在软件代码生成方面表现优异，但在模拟EDA（电子设计自动化）领域应用受限。直接提示LLMs和多模态模型会产生幻觉，无法生成能通过严格SPICE仿真的电路原理图，主要原因是LLMs缺乏对电路拓扑结构的理解且训练数据有限。

Method: 提出ATLAS端到端多步LLM智能体框架，包含：(1) 利用专家知识指导LLM进行规划、选择、参数化和迭代修改；(2) 引入模板约束生成（Template-Constrained Generation），不同于其他基于模板的方法，该方法构建更泛化的SAR ADC生成流程；(3) 多步智能体协同工作，确保满足模拟设计的刚性约束。

Result: 成功生成能够通过SPICE仿真验证的功能性SAR ADC，并在不同工艺节点和输入规格下展示了强大的概念验证效果。

Conclusion: 基于专家知识的多步智能体ATLAS框架为将LLMs集成到可靠的模拟设计方法中建立了实用基础，证明了LLM在模拟EDA领域应用的可行性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant capability in software code generation, their application to analog Electronic Design Automation (EDA) is bottlenecked. Owing to limited circuit topology understanding and data, directly prompting LLMs and multimodal models leads to hallucinations and failure to produce schematics capable of passing rigorous SPICE simulations, as we show in our work. Instead, we propose an end-to-end, multi-step LLM agentic framework ATLAS, capable of generating a functional Successive Approximation Register (SAR) Analog-to-Digital Converter (ADC) that successfully passes simulation validation. To adhere to the rigid constraints of analog design, we utilize expert knowledge to ground the LLM in its planning, selection, parameterization, and iterative modification. As part of ATLAS, we introduce Template-Constrained Generation - which unlike other template-based works - builds towards a more generalized SAR ADC generation flow. We demonstrate a strong proof-of-concept of our framework by developing SAR ADCs across technology nodes and input specs. Overall, our expert-knowledge grounded multi-step agentic ATLAS establishes a pragmatic foundation for integrating LLMs into reliable analog design methodologies.

</details>


### [172] [Stop Means Stop: Measuring and Repairing the Enforcement Gap in Agent-Framework Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14166)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对6个主流开源LLM-agent框架的测试发现，所有框架都存在控制原语（如人工审批门控、运行取消、执行超时）的屏障语义漏洞：本应阻止的副作用在门控暂停期间通过兄弟分支、重放、取消孤儿或超时僵尸等方式泄露。为此提出SOUNDGATE——一个Rust实现的环境外部效应门控，通过内核强制路由确保"持有直到决定、拒绝即取消、重放去重、取消隔离"四个核心属性，并通过形式化验证和差分一致性测试证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent框架提供的控制原语（人工审批门控、取消、超时）声称具有屏障语义——在暂停、取消或超时期间，被门控的副作用不会执行。但这一隐含契约是否真正成立从未被系统性验证，而一旦失效将导致严重的安全后果。

Method: 1) 使用无模型差分探针对6个框架进行漏洞探测；2) 发现5类漏洞（兄弟分支泄露、重放双执行、取消孤儿、超时僵尸、审批门控泄露）；3) 设计和实现SOUNDGATE，一个环境外部效应门控；4) 使用Verus和TLA+进行形式化验证，Loom进行模型检查；5) 通过差分一致性测试（1.2e7次操作）弥合模型与代码的差距。

Result: 1) 6个框架全部存在控制原语的屏障语义漏洞；2) 在固定协议下，前沿模型生成触发漏洞的计划形状的汇集率高达14%；3) 在真实模型驱动未修改框架的审批暂停测试中，1200次运行中有215次在暂停期间执行了副作用；4) SOUNDGATE成功阻止了所有端到端漏洞，释放了合法的副作用；5) 每次写入约1ms准入时间，每秒1.2万至2.6万次持久准入。

Conclusion: 现有LLM-agent框架的控制原语屏障语义普遍不可靠，存在严重安全风险。SOUNDGATE通过环境外部强制门控和形式化验证，能够有效修复这些漏洞，同时保持可接受的性能开销。

Abstract: Production LLM-agent frameworks expose control primitives -- human-in-the-loop approval gates, run cancellation, and execution timeouts -- whose names and documentation imply barrier semantics: while a run is paused, cancelled, or timed out, no gated side effect executes. We show this implied contract holds on none of the six widely used open-source frameworks we test. Model-free differential probes isolate a recurring sibling leak -- an approval gate suspends its own branch while a sibling branch's effect executes during the pause, so a later rejection cannot prevent it -- in every framework shipping a pre-execution gate (five of six), plus three further gaps: replay double-execution, cancellation orphans, and timeout zombies. The hazard is reachable, not merely constructible: under an a-priori-fixed protocol, frontier models emit the leak-triggering plan shape at pooled rates up to 14%, and when live models drive the unmodified frameworks under an approval pause, 215 of 1,200 runs execute their effect during the pause, across three schedulers and two language runtimes. To repair the measured gaps we present SOUNDGATE, an environment-external effect gate in Rust through which every side effect must be admitted, enforcing hold-until-decided, reject-cancels, dedup-on-replay, and fence-on-cancel -- one property per violation class -- under a stated complete-mediation contract discharged for network egress by kernel-enforced routes. We verify the properties over a model of the admission core (Verus; TLA+/TLC, exhaustive to 7.5e7 states; TLAPS), model-check the deployed Rust with Loom, and bridge model to code by differential conformance over 1.2e7 operations -- refinement evidence, not a mechanized proof. SOUNDGATE blocks every measured violation end-to-end on all six frameworks while releasing legitimate effects, at about 1 ms admission per write and 12k-26k durable admissions per second.

</details>


### [173] [Structured Feedback Improves Repair in an LLM Agent Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14167)
*Jaideep Ray,Ankit Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出VeriHarness框架，通过外部验证器控制智能体循环；实验发现包含"失败位置、观测值、可接受替代方案"三字段的反馈将终端成功率提升44/42个百分点（Qwen2.5-Coder-14B/Llama-3.1-8B），其中可接受替代方案贡献大部分增益，JSON格式与散文表述效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部验证拒绝候选方案后，验证环节与下一模型调用之间的接口规范不明确，缺乏系统化的反馈机制设计研究。

Method: 提出VeriHarness框架（代码控制的智能体循环），模型生成候选方案，外部验证器控制接受、预算和追踪；在50对TextWorld游戏中比较原始诊断与含失败位置、观测值、可接受替代方案三字段的反馈效果，进行消融实验及JSON/散文格式对比。

Result: 三字段反馈使Qwen2.5-Coder-14B终端成功率从14/50升至36/50（+44个百分点），Llama-3.1-8B从8/50升至29/50（+42个百分点）；消融显示增益主要来自可接受替代方案；JSON与散文格式成功率接近。

Conclusion: 结构化反馈中可接受替代方案是关键信息，格式（JSON vs 散文）不影响修复效果，该结论在不同调用预算和采样解码设置下一致。

Abstract: LLM agents often retry after external validation rejects a candidate, but the interface between validation and the next model call remains underspecified. We introduce VeriHarness, a code-controlled agent loop in which models generate candidates while external validators control acceptance, budgets, and traces. We use it to compare raw diagnostics with feedback that identifies the failure location, observed value, and admissible alternatives. Across 50 paired TextWorld games under a four-call cap, feedback containing all three fields raises terminal success from 14/50 to 36/50 for Qwen2.5-Coder-14B (+44 percentage points) and from 8/50 to 29/50 for Llama-3.1-8B (+42 points). Ablations locate most of the gain in the admissible alternatives: feedback containing only the location and observed value remains near the raw diagnostic baseline. Presenting the complete repair information in prose instead of a keyed JSON record yields nearly the same success, providing no evidence that JSON syntax itself improves repair. The ordering persists across the tested call budgets and one sampled-decoding setting.

</details>


### [174] [Quantize with Confidence? An Empirical Study of Quantization for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14181)
*Saima Afrin,Md. Zahidul Haque,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种量化方法（GPTQ、AWQ、QuIP#、AQLM、BitsAndBytes、GGUF）在两种大代码模型（Qwen2.5-Coder、CodeLlama）上的效果，使用McEval和CoderEval基准测试，从功能性正确率、可维护性、可靠性、安全性和结构复杂度等维度进行分析，并引入基于Shannon熵和token长度的提示复杂度鲁棒性分析。结果显示AQLM可匹配甚至超越全精度基线，QuIP#在复杂提示下退化最严重，安全性在各条件下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在Ollama等本地推理框架上运行大代码模型时，后训练量化虽能降低内存占用，但其对生成代码质量的影响尚缺乏充分理解，需要系统性的实证研究来指导量化策略选择。

Method: 选取六种SOTA量化方法（GPTQ、AWQ、QuIP#、AQLM、BitsAndBytes、GGUF）和两个代表性代码模型族（Qwen2.5-Coder、CodeLlama），在McEval和CoderEval多语言基准上评估pass@1功能性正确率及可维护性、可靠性、安全性和结构复杂度，并引入基于Shannon熵和token长度的提示复杂度鲁棒性分析。

Result: 不同量化方法对正确性和代码质量的影响存在显著差异：AQLM一致性匹配或超越全精度基线；QuIP#正确性退化最严重，尤其在复杂提示下；安全属性在不同模型、基准和编程语言间保持稳定；对提示复杂度的鲁棒性因方法而异。

Conclusion: 研究为在资源受限硬件上部署大代码模型时的量化策略选择提供了实用指导，并强调评估量化模型时应超越功能性正确率，关注更广泛的代码质量维度。

Abstract: The growing adoption of local inference frameworks such as Ollama has made it increasingly common for developers to run large code models on laptops and other resource-constrained hardware. In these settings, post-training quantization is essential for reducing memory footprint and enabling practical deployment, yet its impact on generated code remains insufficiently understood.
